$$

<sub>w</sub>h<sub>ere</sub> $p _ { ( 1 ) }$ <sub>an</sub>d $p _ { ( 2 ) }$ d<sub>eno</sub>t<sub>e</sub> th<sub>e</sub> t<sub>wo</sub> l<sub>arges</sub>t h<sub>ypo</sub>th<sub>es</sub>i<sub>s pro</sub>b<sub>a-</sub> biliti<sub>es, an</sub>d $\begin{array} { r } { \overline { { \kappa } } ^ { ( t ) } = \frac { 1 } { K _ { t } } \sum _ { i = 1 } ^ { K _ { t } } \kappa _ { i } ^ { ( t ) } } \end{array}$ <sub>.</sub> Th<sub>e va</sub>l<sub>ue</sub> f<sub>unc</sub>ti<sub>on</sub> $V _ { \nu } ( \mathbf { d } _ { t } )$ <sub>es</sub>ti<sub>ma</sub>t<sub>es</sub> th<sub>e expec</sub>t<sub>e</sub>d <sub>ar</sub>bit<sub>ra</sub>ti<sub>on qua</sub>lit<sub>y</sub> f<sub>rom e</sub>f<sub>ec</sub>ti<sub>ve ev</sub>i<sub>-</sub> d<sub>ence</sub> <sub>quan</sub>tit<sub>y,</sub> <sub>ass</sub>i<sub>gnmen</sub>t <sub>con</sub>fid<sub>ence,</sub> <sub>an</sub>d <sub>pos</sub>t<sub>er</sub>i<sub>or</sub> <sub>uncer-</sub> t<sub>a</sub>i<sub>n</sub>t<sub>y.</sub> Th<sub>e ac</sub>t<sub>or uses</sub> th<sub>e con</sub>t<sub>ex</sub>t<sub>ua</sub>l <sub>s</sub>t<sub>a</sub>t<sub>e represen</sub>t<sub>a</sub>ti<sub>on</sub> t<sub>o</sub> <sub>se</sub>l<sub>ec</sub>t <sub>recovery ac</sub>ti<sub>ons.</sub> Gi<sub>ven re</sub>t<sub>urn</sub> $\begin{array} { r } { G _ { t } = \sum _ { k \geq 0 } \gamma ^ { k } R _ { t + k } } \end{array}$ <sub>an</sub>d <sub>a</sub>d<sub>van</sub>t<sub>age es</sub>ti<sub>ma</sub>t<sub>e</sub> $\widehat { A } _ { t } = G _ { t } - V _ { \nu } ( \mathbf { d } _ { t } )$ <sub>,</sub> th<sub>e po</sub>li<sub>cy an</sub>d value function are optimized with an actor–critic objective:

$$
\mathcal { L } _ { \mathrm { A } } = - \mathbb { E } _ { t } \left[ \log \pi _ { \omega } ( A _ { t } \mid S _ { t } ) \operatorname { s g } ( \widehat { A } _ { t } ) \right] ,
$$

$$
\mathcal { L } _ { \mathrm { V } } = \mathbb { E } _ { t } \left[ \left( V _ { \nu } ( \mathbf { d } _ { t } ) - \mathrm { s g } ( G _ { t } ) \right) ^ { 2 } \right] ,\tag{11}
$$

$$
\mathcal { L } _ { \mathrm { R L } } = \mathcal { L } _ { \mathrm { A } } + c _ { v } \mathcal { L } _ { \mathrm { V } } - c _ { e } \mathbb { E } _ { t } [ H ( \pi _ { \omega } ( \cdot \mid S _ { t } ) ) ] ,\tag{12}
$$

(13)

$$

<sub>w</sub>h<sub>ere</sub> $h ^ { * }$ d<sub>eno</sub>t<sub>es</sub> th<sub>e groun</sub>d<sub>-</sub>t<sub>ru</sub>th <sub>conc</sub>l<sub>us</sub>i<sub>on.</sub> T<sub>o</sub> l<sub>everage</sub> <sub>provenance</sub> i<sub>n</sub>f<sub>orma</sub>ti<sub>on, a wea</sub>kl<sub>y superv</sub>i<sub>se</sub>d <sub>con</sub>t<sub>ras</sub>ti<sub>ve o</sub>b<sub>-</sub> jective is introduced over evidence assignments. For each anchor memory i, $\mathcal { P } ( i )$ <sub>an</sub>d $\mathcal { N } ( i )$ d<sub>eno</sub>t<sub>e memor</sub>i<sub>es w</sub>ith <sub>o</sub>b<sub>serve</sub>d <sub>provenance re</sub>l<sub>a</sub>ti<sub>ons an</sub>d di<sub>s</sub>ti<sub>nc</sub>t <sub>source or</sub>i<sub>g</sub>i<sub>ns,</sub> <sub>respec</sub>ti<sub>ve</sub>l<sub>y.</sub> Si<sub>nce</sub> <sub>provenance</sub> <sub>prov</sub>id<sub>es</sub> <sub>par</sub>ti<sub>a</sub>l d<sub>epen</sub>d<sub>ency</sub> <sub>cues,</sub> it i<sub>s use</sub>d <sub>as wea</sub>k <sub>superv</sub>i<sub>s</sub>i<sub>on ra</sub>th<sub>er</sub> th<sub>an a</sub> h<sub>ar</sub>d <sub>ass</sub>i<sub>gn-</sub> <sub>men</sub>t <sub>cons</sub>t<sub>ra</sub>i<sub>n</sub>t<sub>.</sub> Th<sub>e</sub> d<sub>epen</sub>d<sub>ence</sub> l<sub>oss</sub> i<sub>s</sub> d<sub>e</sub>fi<sub>ne</sub>d <sub>as:</sub>

$$
\mathcal { L } _ { \mathrm { d } } = - \mathbb { E } _ { i \sim B } \mathbb { E } _ { j \sim \mathcal { P } ( i ) } \log \frac { \exp ( r _ { i j } ^ { ( T ) } / \tau _ { d } ) } { \sum _ { k \in \mathcal { P } ( i ) \cup \mathcal { N } ( i ) } \exp ( r _ { i k } ^ { ( T ) } / \tau _ { d } ) } .\tag{15}
$$

<sub>w</sub>h<sub>ere</sub> $r _ { i j } ^ { ( T ) } = \langle \mathbf { z } _ { i } ^ { ( T ) } , \mathbf { z } _ { j } ^ { ( T ) } \rangle$ <sub>measures</sub> th<sub>e over</sub>l<sub>ap</sub> b<sub>e</sub>t<sub>ween</sub> th<sub>e</sub> source assignment distributions of memories i and $j ,$ <sub>an</sub>d $\tau _ { d }$ i<sub>s</sub> th<sub>e</sub> t<sub>empera</sub>t<sub>ure</sub> <sub>parame</sub>t<sub>er.</sub> P<sub>rovenance-</sub>b<sub>ase</sub>d <sub>con</sub>t<sub>ras</sub>ti<sub>ve</sub> <sub>superv</sub>i<sub>s</sub>i<sub>on</sub> <sub>regu</sub>l<sub>ar</sub>i<sub>zes</sub> <sub>ev</sub>id<sub>ence</sub> <sub>ass</sub>i<sub>gnmen</sub>t<sub>s</sub> <sub>w</sub>ith<sub>ou</sub>t <sub>re</sub>l<sub>y-</sub> i<sub>ng on seman</sub>ti<sub>c s</sub>i<sub>m</sub>il<sub>ar</sub>it<sub>y as a proxy</sub> f<sub>or</sub> d<sub>epen</sub>d<sub>ency.</sub> Th<sub>e</sub> overall training objective is

$$
\begin{array} { r } { \mathcal { L } = \mathcal { L } _ { \mathrm { R L } } + \lambda _ { \mathrm { t a s k } } \mathcal { L } _ { \mathrm { t a s k } } + \lambda _ { \mathrm { d } } \mathcal { L } _ { \mathrm { d } } . } \end{array}\tag{16}
$$

## Experiments

## Experimental Setup

Datasets We evaluate CAMA on three long-term memory benchmarks: MemoryAgentBench (Hu, Wang, and McAule<sub>y</sub> 2026) evaluates LLM a<sub>g</sub>ent memor<sub>y</sub> ca<sub>p</sub>abilities th<sub>roug</sub>h i<sub>ncremen</sub>t<sub>a</sub>l <sub>mu</sub>lti<sub>-</sub>t<sub>urn</sub> i<sub>n</sub>t<sub>erac</sub>ti<sub>ons,</sub> i<sub>nc</sub>l<sub>u</sub>di<sub>ng se-</sub> lective forgetting under conflicting memory updates. Long-MemEval (Wu et al. 2025) evaluates long-term memory <sub>re</sub>t<sub>r</sub>i<sub>eva</sub>l <sub>an</sub>d <sub>reason</sub>i<sub>ng</sub> i<sub>n</sub> <sub>conversa</sub>ti<sub>ona</sub>l <sub>agen</sub>t<sub>s</sub> <sub>un</sub>d<sub>er</sub> <sub>ex-</sub> tended interaction histories. Evaluation Metrics We adopt two complementary categories of metrics for evaluation. For Task-Level Performance, we follow the standard evaluation protocols of each b<sub>enc</sub>h<sub>mar</sub>k<sub>.</sub> S<sub>pec</sub>ifi<sub>ca</sub>ll<sub>y, on</sub> M<sub>emory</sub>A<sub>gen</sub>tB<sub>enc</sub>h<sub>, we re-</sub> port the performance on Fact Consolidation tasks, including Single-Hop Fact Consolidation (FC-SH), Multi-Hop Fact Consolidation (FC-MH), and the overall score. On Long-MemEval and LoCoMo, we re<sub>p</sub>ort Exact Match (EM), F1, BERTScore, and judge-based evaluation scores. For Memory Correlation Bias, we evaluate evidence arbitration under correlation-aware settings using four metrics: (i) Correct Minority Recovery (CMR), which measures the proportion <sub>o</sub>f <sub>cases w</sub>h<sub>ere</sub> th<sub>e mo</sub>d<sub>e</sub>l <sub>recovers</sub> th<sub>e correc</sub>t <sub>answer w</sub>h<sub>en</sub> <sub>suppor</sub>ti<sub>ng</sub> <sub>ev</sub>id<sub>ence</sub> i<sub>s</sub> <sub>ou</sub>t<sub>num</sub>b<sub>ere</sub>d b<sub>y</sub> <sub>corre</sub>l<sub>a</sub>t<sub>e</sub>d <sub>memor</sub>i<sub>es;</sub> (ii) Replication Sensitivity (RS), which measures the impact <sub>o</sub>f i<sub>ncreas</sub>i<sub>ng re</sub>d<sub>un</sub>d<sub>an</sub>t <sub>memor</sub>i<sub>es</sub> f<sub>rom</sub> th<sub>e same ev</sub>id<sub>ence</sub> source on model decisions; (iii) Independent Evidence Gain (IEG), which quantifies the benefit of incor<sub>p</sub>oratin<sub>g</sub> additional independent evidence; and (iv) Evidence Resolution Rate (ERR), which measures the proportion of evidencei<sub>nsu</sub>fi<sub>c</sub>i<sub>en</sub>t i<sub>ns</sub>t<sub>ances</sub> th<sub>a</sub>t <sub>are</sub> <sub>correc</sub>tl<sub>y</sub> <sub>reso</sub>l<sub>ve</sub>d<sub>.</sub>

Implementation Details We conduct our experiments with Dee<sub>p</sub>Seek-V4-Flash (Dee<sub>p</sub>Seek 2026) and Qwen3.6- 27B (Qwen Team 2026) as the backbone LLMs. All baseline <sub>me</sub>th<sub>o</sub>d<sub>s an</sub>d CAMA <sub>use</sub> th<sub>e same</sub> b<sub>ac</sub>kb<sub>one mo</sub>d<sub>e</sub>l<sub>s, mem-</sub> <sub>ory poo</sub>l<sub>s, an</sub>d <sub>re</sub>t<sub>r</sub>i<sub>eva</sub>l <sub>se</sub>tti<sub>ngs</sub> f<sub>or</sub> f<sub>a</sub>i<sub>r compar</sub>i<sub>son.</sub> Th<sub>e</sub> <sub>ev</sub>id<sub>ence</sub> <sub>ass</sub>i<sub>gnmen</sub>t <sub>mo</sub>d<sub>u</sub>l<sub>e</sub> i<sub>s</sub> t<sub>ra</sub>i<sub>ne</sub>d <sub>on</sub> th<sub>e</sub> t<sub>ra</sub>i<sub>n</sub>i<sub>ng</sub> <sub>sp</sub>lit <sub>an</sub>d f<sub>rozen</sub> d<sub>ur</sub>i<sub>ng</sub> <sub>eva</sub>l<sub>ua</sub>ti<sub>on,</sub> <sub>w</sub>hil<sub>e</sub> th<sub>e</sub> <sub>recovery</sub> <sub>po</sub>li<sub>cy</sub> i<sub>s</sub> <sub>op</sub>ti<sub>m</sub>i<sub>ze</sub>d d<sub>ur</sub>i<sub>ng</sub> t<sub>ra</sub>i<sub>n</sub>i<sub>ng</sub> <sub>an</sub>d fi<sub>xe</sub>d d<sub>ur</sub>i<sub>ng</sub> i<sub>n</sub>f<sub>erence.</sub> W<sub>e</sub> <sub>se</sub>t th<sub>e num</sub>b<sub>er o</sub>f <sub>re</sub>t<sub>r</sub>i<sub>eve</sub>d <sub>memor</sub>i<sub>es</sub> t<sub>o</sub> $K = 1 0 .$ <sub>,</sub> th<sub>e</sub> <sub>num</sub>b<sub>er</sub> of latent evidence slots to J = 6, and the recovery budget to B = 3, respectively. For evidence estimation, we set the Hill diversity order to α = 2 and the provenance prior strength to µ = 0.5. For recovery policy optimization, we use a discount factor of γ = 0.95, a value coeficient of $c _ { v } = 0 . 5$ <sub>,</sub> <sub>an</sub>d <sub>an</sub> <sub>en</sub>t<sub>ropy coe</sub>fi<sub>c</sub>i<sub>en</sub>t <sub>o</sub>f $c _ { e } = 0 . 0 1$ b<sub>y</sub> d<sub>e</sub>f<sub>au</sub>lt<sub>.</sub>

• Majority Voting (Wan<sub>g</sub> et al. 2023) re<sub>p</sub>resents <sub>consensus-</sub>b<sub>ase</sub>d <sub>ev</sub>id<sub>ence</sub> <sub>aggrega</sub>ti<sub>on</sub> <sub>s</sub>t<sub>ra</sub>t<sub>eg</sub>i<sub>es.</sub> It <sub>ag-</sub> <sub>grega</sub>t<sub>es</sub> <sub>re</sub>t<sub>r</sub>i<sub>eve</sub>d <sub>memor</sub>i<sub>es</sub> b<sub>y</sub> <sub>se</sub>l<sub>ec</sub>ti<sub>ng</sub> th<sub>e</sub> h<sub>ypo</sub>th<sub>es</sub>i<sub>s</sub> supported by the majority of memory entries, implicitl<sub>y assum</sub>i<sub>ng</sub> th<sub>a</sub>t <sub>eac</sub>h <sub>memory prov</sub>id<sub>es an</sub> i<sub>n</sub>d<sub>epen</sub>d<sub>en</sub>t <sub>vo</sub>t<sub>e.</sub> Alth<sub>oug</sub>h <sub>e</sub>f<sub>ec</sub>ti<sub>ve w</sub>h<sub>en ev</sub>id<sub>ence sources are</sub> i<sub>n</sub>d<sub>e-</sub> pendent, such strategies may sufer from false majorities <sub>w</sub>h<sub>en mu</sub>lti<sub>p</sub>l<sub>e memor</sub>i<sub>es or</sub>i<sub>g</sub>i<sub>na</sub>t<sub>e</sub> f<sub>rom</sub> th<sub>e same un</sub>d<sub>er-</sub> l<sub>y</sub>i<sub>ng ev</sub>id<sub>ence source.</sub>

• Mem0 (Chhikara et al. 2025) is a lon<sub>g</sub>-term memor<sub>y</sub> <sub>managemen</sub>t f<sub>ramewor</sub>k th<sub>a</sub>t <sub>ex</sub>t<sub>rac</sub>t<sub>s sa</sub>li<sub>en</sub>t i<sub>n</sub>f<sub>orma</sub>ti<sub>on</sub> f<sub>rom</sub> i<sub>n</sub>t<sub>erac</sub>ti<sub>ons</sub> <sub>an</sub>d <sub>ma</sub>i<sub>n</sub>t<sub>a</sub>i<sub>ns</sub> <sub>a</sub> <sub>compac</sub>t <sub>memory</sub> <sub>s</sub>t<sub>ore</sub> th<sub>roug</sub>h <sub>memory</sub> <sub>a</sub>dditi<sub>on,</sub> <sub>up</sub>d<sub>a</sub>ti<sub>ng,</sub> <sub>an</sub>d <sub>conso</sub>lid<sub>a</sub>ti<sub>on.</sub> U<sub>n</sub>lik<sub>e</sub> di<sub>rec</sub>t <sub>re</sub>t<sub>r</sub>i<sub>eva</sub>l <sub>me</sub>th<sub>o</sub>d<sub>s,</sub> M<sub>em</sub>0 f<sub>ocuses on sca</sub>l<sub>-</sub> <sub>a</sub>bl<sub>e</sub> <sub>memory</sub> <sub>organ</sub>i<sub>za</sub>ti<sub>on</sub> <sub>an</sub>d <sub>a</sub>d<sub>ap</sub>ti<sub>ve</sub> <sub>memory</sub> <sub>ma</sub>i<sub>n</sub>t<sub>e-</sub> n<sub>a</sub>n<sub>ce</sub> f<sub>o</sub>r l<sub>o</sub>n<sub>g-</sub>r<sub>u</sub>nnin<sub>g</sub> LLM <sub>age</sub>nt<sub>s.</sub> W<sub>e</sub> in<sub>c</sub>l<sub>u</sub>d<sub>e</sub> M<sub>e</sub>m0 t<sub>o</sub> <sub>eva</sub>l<sub>ua</sub>t<sub>e</sub> <sub>w</sub>h<sub>e</sub>th<sub>er</sub> <sub>ex</sub>i<sub>s</sub>ti<sub>ng</sub> <sub>memory</sub> <sub>managemen</sub>t <sub>s</sub>t<sub>ra</sub>t<sub>eg</sub>i<sub>es</sub> <sub>can m</sub>iti<sub>ga</sub>t<sub>e corre</sub>l<sub>a</sub>ti<sub>on</sub> i<sub>ssues</sub> th<sub>roug</sub>h <sub>memory conso</sub>li<sub>-</sub> d<sub>a</sub>ti<sub>on.</sub>

We include MAD to evaluate whether <sub>genera</sub>l <sub>mu</sub>lti<sub>-agen</sub>t <sub>co</sub>ll<sub>a</sub>b<sub>ora</sub>ti<sub>on</sub> <sub>can</sub> <sub>reso</sub>l<sub>ve</sub> <sub>corre</sub>l<sub>a</sub>t<sub>e</sub>d <sub>or con</sub>fli<sub>c</sub>ti<sub>ng memor</sub>i<sub>es</sub> th<sub>roug</sub>h <sub>agen</sub>t i<sub>n</sub>t<sub>erac</sub>ti<sub>ons.</sub>

## Metric Descriptions

## Task-Level Performance

W<sub>e eva</sub>l<sub>ua</sub>t<sub>e</sub> th<sub>e overa</sub>ll t<sub>as</sub>k<sub>-so</sub>l<sub>v</sub>i<sub>ng capa</sub>bilit<sub>y o</sub>f CAMA f<sub>o</sub>l<sub>-</sub> l<sub>ow</sub>i<sub>ng</sub> th<sub>e s</sub>t<sub>an</sub>d<sub>ar</sub>d <sub>eva</sub>l<sub>ua</sub>ti<sub>on pro</sub>t<sub>oco</sub>l<sub>s o</sub>f <sub>eac</sub>h b<sub>enc</sub>h<sub>mar</sub>k<sub>.</sub> For MemoryAgentBench, we focus on the Fact Consolidation tasks, including Single-Hop Fact Consolidation (FC-SH), Multi-Hop Fact Consolidation (FC-MH), and the over-<sub>a</sub>ll <sub>score.</sub> Th<sub>ese</sub> <sub>me</sub>t<sub>r</sub>i<sub>cs</sub> <sub>eva</sub>l<sub>ua</sub>t<sub>e</sub> <sub>w</sub>h<sub>e</sub>th<sub>er</sub> <sub>an</sub> <sub>agen</sub>t <sub>can</sub> <sub>cor-</sub> <sub>rec</sub>tl<sub>y conso</sub>lid<sub>a</sub>t<sub>e</sub> f<sub>ac</sub>t<sub>ua</sub>l i<sub>n</sub>f<sub>orma</sub>ti<sub>on</sub> f<sub>rom evo</sub>l<sub>v</sub>i<sub>ng mu</sub>lti<sub>-</sub> turn interactions. For LongMemEval and LoCoMo, we re-<sub>p</sub>ort Exact Match (EM), F1 score, BERTScore, and jud<sub>g</sub>eb<sub>ase</sub>d <sub>eva</sub>l<sub>ua</sub>ti<sub>on scores</sub> f<sub>o</sub>ll<sub>ow</sub>i<sub>ng</sub> th<sub>e</sub>i<sub>r or</sub>i<sub>g</sub>i<sub>na</sub>l <sub>eva</sub>l<sub>ua</sub>ti<sub>on</sub> <sub>pro</sub>t<sub>oco</sub>l<sub>s.</sub> Th<sub>ese</sub> <sub>me</sub>t<sub>r</sub>i<sub>cs</sub> <sub>eva</sub>l<sub>ua</sub>t<sub>e</sub> <sub>answer</sub> <sub>correc</sub>t<sub>ness,</sub> <sub>se-</sub> <sub>man</sub>ti<sub>c s</sub>i<sub>m</sub>il<sub>ar</sub>it<sub>y, an</sub>d <sub>overa</sub>ll <sub>response qua</sub>lit<sub>y</sub> i<sub>n</sub> l<sub>ong-</sub>t<sub>erm</sub> <sub>conversa</sub>ti<sub>ona</sub>l <sub>memory</sub> <sub>reason</sub>i<sub>ng.</sub>

## Memory Correlation Bias Evaluation

T<sub>o eva</sub>l<sub>ua</sub>t<sub>e w</sub>h<sub>e</sub>th<sub>er an agen</sub>t <sub>can e</sub>f<sub>ec</sub>ti<sub>ve</sub>l<sub>y ar</sub>bit<sub>ra</sub>t<sub>e ev</sub>i<sub>-</sub> d<sub>ence un</sub>d<sub>er corre</sub>l<sub>a</sub>t<sub>e</sub>d <sub>memor</sub>i<sub>es, we cons</sub>t<sub>ruc</sub>t <sub>corre</sub>l<sub>a</sub>ti<sub>on-</sub> <sub>aware eva</sub>l<sub>ua</sub>ti<sub>on se</sub>tti<sub>ngs an</sub>d i<sub>n</sub>t<sub>ro</sub>d<sub>uce</sub> f<sub>our comp</sub>l<sub>emen</sub>t<sub>ary</sub> <sub>me</sub>t<sub>r</sub>i<sub>cs.</sub> L<sub>e</sub>t $\mathcal { C } _ { q } ^ { ( t ) }$ denote the memory slice after t recovery <sub>s</sub>t<sub>eps, an</sub>d l<sub>e</sub>t $P ^ { ( t ) } ( h | q , \mathcal { C } _ { q } ^ { ( t ) } )$ d<sub>eno</sub>t<sub>e</sub> th<sub>e ar</sub>bit<sub>ra</sub>ti<sub>on pos</sub>t<sub>er</sub>i<sub>or</sub> <sub>over</sub> <sub>can</sub>did<sub>a</sub>t<sub>e</sub> h<sub>ypo</sub>th<sub>eses.</sub>

Correct Minority Recovery (CMR). CMR evaluates <sub>w</sub>h<sub>e</sub>th<sub>er</sub> th<sub>e mo</sub>d<sub>e</sub>l <sub>can recover</sub> th<sub>e correc</sub>t <sub>answer w</sub>h<sub>en</sub> th<sub>e</sub> <sub>num</sub>b<sub>er</sub> <sub>o</sub>f <sub>corre</sub>l<sub>a</sub>t<sub>e</sub>d <sub>memory</sub> <sub>en</sub>t<sub>r</sub>i<sub>es</sub> <sub>suppor</sub>ti<sub>ng</sub> <sub>an</sub> i<sub>ncor-</sub> <sub>rec</sub>t h<sub>ypo</sub>th<sub>es</sub>i<sub>s</sub> <sub>excee</sub>d<sub>s</sub> th<sub>e</sub> <sub>num</sub>b<sub>er</sub> <sub>o</sub>f i<sub>n</sub>d<sub>epen</sub>d<sub>en</sub>t <sub>memor</sub>i<sub>es</sub> <sub>suppor</sub>ti<sub>ng</sub> th<sub>e</sub> <sub>correc</sub>t h<sub>ypo</sub>th<sub>es</sub>i<sub>s.</sub> S<sub>pec</sub>ifi<sub>ca</sub>ll<sub>y,</sub> it <sub>measures</sub> th<sub>e</sub> <sub>propor</sub>ti<sub>on</sub> <sub>o</sub>f <sub>suc</sub>h <sub>m</sub>i<sub>nor</sub>it<sub>y-suppor</sub>t <sub>cases</sub> <sub>w</sub>h<sub>ere</sub> th<sub>e</sub> fi<sub>na</sub>l <sub>pre</sub>di<sub>c</sub>ti<sub>on rema</sub>i<sub>ns correc</sub>t<sub>:</sub>

$$
\mathrm { C M R } = \frac { 1 } { | \mathcal { D } _ { \mathrm { m i n o r } } | } \sum _ { q \in \mathcal { D } _ { \mathrm { m i n o r } } } \mathbb { I } \left[ \hat { h } _ { q } ^ { ( T ) } = h _ { q } ^ { * } \right] \times 1 0 0 \% ,\tag{18}
$$

<sub>w</sub>h<sub>ere</sub> ${ \mathcal { D } } _ { \mathrm { m i n o r } }$ d<sub>eno</sub>t<sub>es</sub> th<sub>e su</sub>b<sub>se</sub>t <sub>o</sub>f i<sub>ns</sub>t<sub>ances w</sub>ith <sub>m</sub>i<sub>nor-</sub> it<sub>y</sub> <sub>correc</sub>t <sub>ev</sub>id<sub>ence</sub> <sub>an</sub>d $\hat { h } _ { q } ^ { ( T ) }$ d<sub>eno</sub>t<sub>es</sub> th<sub>e</sub> fi<sub>na</sub>l <sub>pre</sub>di<sub>c</sub>ti<sub>on</sub> <sub>a</sub>ft<sub>er ar</sub>bit<sub>ra</sub>ti<sub>on.</sub>

Replication Sensitivity (RS). RS measures the sensitivity <sub>o</sub>f <sub>mo</sub>d<sub>e</sub>l d<sub>ec</sub>i<sub>s</sub>i<sub>ons</sub> t<sub>o</sub> th<sub>e rep</sub>li<sub>ca</sub>ti<sub>on o</sub>f <sub>corre</sub>l<sub>a</sub>t<sub>e</sub>d <sub>memor</sub>i<sub>es</sub> f<sub>rom</sub> th<sub>e</sub> <sub>same</sub> <sub>ev</sub>id<sub>ence</sub> <sub>source.</sub> Gi<sub>ven</sub> <sub>an</sub> <sub>or</sub>i<sub>g</sub>i<sub>na</sub>l <sub>memory</sub> <sub>s</sub>li<sub>ce</sub> $\mathcal { C } _ { q }$ <sub>an</sub>d it<sub>s corre</sub>l<sub>a</sub>ti<sub>on-augmen</sub>t<sub>e</sub>d <sub>vers</sub>i<sub>on</sub> $\widetilde { \mathcal { C } } _ { q } .$ <sub>,</sub> RS i<sub>s</sub> d<sub>e-</sub> fi<sub>ne</sub>d <sub>as:</sub>

$$
\mathrm { R S } = \frac { 1 } { | \mathcal { D } | } \sum _ { q \in \mathcal { D } } \mathbb { I } \left[ \hat { h } ( \mathcal { C } _ { q } ) \neq \hat { h } ( \widetilde { \mathcal { C } } _ { q } ) \right] \times 1 0 0 \% ,\tag{19}
$$

<sub>w</sub>h<sub>ere a</sub> l<sub>ower</sub> RS i<sub>n</sub>di<sub>ca</sub>t<sub>es</sub> th<sub>a</sub>t th<sub>e mo</sub>d<sub>e</sub>l i<sub>s</sub> l<sub>ess a</sub>f<sub>ec</sub>t<sub>e</sub>d b<sub>y</sub> <sub>re</sub>d<sub>un</sub>d<sub>an</sub>t <sub>memory</sub> <sub>rep</sub>li<sub>ca</sub>ti<sub>on.</sub>

Independent Evidence Gain (IEG). IEG measures the b<sub>ene</sub>fit <sub>o</sub>f i<sub>n</sub>t<sub>ro</sub>d<sub>uc</sub>i<sub>ng a</sub>dditi<sub>ona</sub>l i<sub>n</sub>d<sub>epen</sub>d<sub>en</sub>t <sub>ev</sub>id<sub>ence</sub> f<sub>rom</sub> di<sub>s</sub>ti<sub>nc</sub>t <sub>sources.</sub> F<sub>or eac</sub>h i<sub>ns</sub>t<sub>ance,</sub> l<sub>e</sub>t $\mathcal { C } _ { q }$ <sub>an</sub>d $\mathcal { C } _ { q } ^ { i n d }$ d<sub>eno</sub>t<sub>e</sub> th<sub>e</sub> <sub>or</sub>i<sub>g</sub>i<sub>na</sub>l <sub>an</sub>d i<sub>n</sub>d<sub>epen</sub>d<sub>en</sub>t<sub>-ev</sub>id<sub>ence-augmen</sub>t<sub>e</sub>d <sub>memory</sub> <sub>s</sub>li<sub>ces,</sub> <sub>respec</sub>ti<sub>ve</sub>l<sub>y.</sub> IEG i<sub>s</sub> d<sub>e</sub>fi<sub>ne</sub>d <sub>as</sub> th<sub>e</sub> <sub>per</sub>f<sub>ormance</sub> i<sub>m-</sub> <sub>provemen</sub>t <sub>a</sub>ft<sub>er</sub> <sub>a</sub>ddi<sub>ng</sub> i<sub>n</sub>d<sub>epen</sub>d<sub>en</sub>t <sub>ev</sub>id<sub>ence:</sub>

$$
\mathrm { I E G } = 1 0 0 \% \times \frac { 1 } { | \mathcal { D } | } \sum _ { q \in \mathcal { D } } \left( \mathbb { I } [ \hat { h } ( \mathcal { C } _ { q } ^ { i n d } ) = h _ { q } ^ { * } ] - \mathbb { I } [ \hat { h } ( \mathcal { C } _ { q } ) = h _ { q } ^ { * } ] \right) .\tag{20}
$$

A l<sub>arger</sub> IEG i<sub>n</sub>di<sub>ca</sub>t<sub>es</sub> th<sub>a</sub>t th<sub>e mo</sub>d<sub>e</sub>l <sub>can e</sub>f<sub>ec</sub>ti<sub>ve</sub>l<sub>y u</sub>tili<sub>ze</sub> <sub>comp</sub>l<sub>emen</sub>t<sub>ary</sub> <sub>ev</sub>id<sub>ence</sub> f<sub>rom</sub> i<sub>n</sub>d<sub>epen</sub>d<sub>en</sub>t <sub>sources.</sub>

Evidence Resolution Rate (ERR). ERR evaluates end-to-<sub>en</sub>d d<sub>ec</sub>i<sub>s</sub>i<sub>on correc</sub>t<sub>ness on</sub> i<sub>ns</sub>t<sub>ances w</sub>h<sub>ose</sub> i<sub>n</sub>iti<sub>a</sub>l <sub>re</sub>t<sub>r</sub>i<sub>eve</sub>d <sub>memory s</sub>li<sub>ces con</sub>t<sub>a</sub>i<sub>n</sub> i<sub>nsu</sub>fi<sub>c</sub>i<sub>en</sub>t i<sub>n</sub>d<sub>epen</sub>d<sub>en</sub>t <sub>ev</sub>id<sub>ence</sub> f<sub>or</sub> <sub>re</sub>li<sub>a</sub>bl<sub>e ar</sub>bit<sub>ra</sub>ti<sub>on.</sub> F<sub>or eac</sub>h <sub>eva</sub>l<sub>ua</sub>t<sub>e</sub>d <sub>me</sub>th<sub>o</sub>d<sub>,</sub> it <sub>measures</sub> th<sub>e</sub> <sub>propor</sub>ti<sub>on</sub> <sub>o</sub>f <sub>suc</sub>h i<sub>ns</sub>t<sub>ances</sub> th<sub>a</sub>t <sub>are</sub> <sub>correc</sub>tl<sub>y</sub> <sub>reso</sub>l<sub>ve</sub>d b<sub>y</sub> th<sub>e me</sub>th<sub>o</sub>d’<sub>s</sub> fi<sub>na</sub>l <sub>pre</sub>di<sub>c</sub>ti<sub>on:</sub>

$$
\mathrm { E R R } \left( \% \right) = \frac { 1 0 0 } { \left| \mathcal { D } _ { \mathrm { r e c } } \right| } \sum _ { q \in \mathcal { D } _ { \mathrm { r e c } } } \mathbb { I } \left[ \hat { h } _ { q } ^ { \mathrm { f i n a l } } = h _ { q } ^ { * } \right] ,\tag{21}
$$

## Additional Experimental Results Memory Correlation Bias Mitigation
